Change: `maxPackageEntries = 65_535` (classic-zip maximum); `KindMismatch { id, envelopeKind, ownKind }`; `kindMismatches` on `ImportParseResult` (always present, empty for legacy formats). No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §6, PROGRESS.md step 3.6 review.
- **2026-09-29, step 3.6 — import part 1: `IMPORT_LIMITS.maxAssetBytes`, `ImportFormat` renamed, `QuarantinedItem` gains `raw`/`rawTruncated`, new types `UnsafeKeyRename`/`DetectFormatResult`/`ParsedAsset`/`RejectedAsset`/`PackageEntryIssue`/`ImportRejectionCode`/`ImportParseResult`/`ImportParseOutcome` (contract changes, reviewer-approved in "Phase 3 contract decisions" Q5, Q6 unsafe-keys part, Q12 import-functions part; the rest recorded here).** Problem: `IMPORT_LIMITS` had no asset-bytes cap (Q5); `ImportFormat`'s legacy variant was named `'legacy-orn-html'`, which names the wrong thing (the legacy app is HTML, but only its exported JSON is ever imported, MIGRATION.md §4.1) and cannot distinguish v6 from v4; `QuarantinedItem` had no `raw`/`rawTruncated`, although PERSISTENCE.md §7 has always required them for import quarantine; parsing a file end to end (caps, safe parse, format detection, `.ornpkg` unpack, migration, per-record L1) had no result type at all. Change: `IMPORT_LIMITS.maxAssetBytes = 20 * 1024 * 1024`; `ImportFormat = 'orn.dataset' | 'orn.package' | 'legacy_v6' | 'legacy_v4'` (`'legacy-orn-html'` was unused, confirmed by grep, so this is additive in effect); `QuarantinedItem` gains `raw: JsonValue` and `rawTruncated?: { originalChars: number }`; `UnsafeKeyRename { path, originalKey }`; `DetectFormatResult`; `ParsedAsset`, `RejectedAsset`, `PackageEntryIssue`; `ImportRejectionCode`; `ImportParseResult` and `ImportParseOutcome` (all `src/io/contracts.ts`). No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §6-§7, PROGRESS.md step 3.6.
- **2026-09-29, step 3.5 — export: `AssetBytesProvider`, `MissingReference`, `ExportResult`, `ExportBuildResult`, `TaskOptions` (contract changes, reviewer-approved in "Phase 3 contract decisions", Q7, part of Q9, part of Q12).** Problem: `ExportOptions` (scope, subset, `includeDependencies`, `assets`) had no way to hand export a source of asset bytes without breaking options' structured-clone-safety, no result type to report bytes/counts/gaps, and no cancel/progress shape that does not require importing `src/workers` (io and persistence must not). Change: `AssetBytesProvider(assetId, sha256) => Promise<Uint8Array | undefined>`; `MissingReference { from, path, targetKind?, targetId }`; `ExportResult { bytes, counts, missingAssets, missingReferences }`; `ExportBuildResult` (`ExportErrorCode` `'CANCELLED' | 'SERIALIZE_FAILED'`); `TaskOptions { signal?, onProgress? }` (all in `src/io/contracts.ts`). No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §8 (scope-closure algorithm spelled out, REVIEW REQUIRED), PROGRESS.md step 3.5.
- **2026-09-28, step 3.4 — writer lock, change channel, follower mode, promotion (contract changes, ADR-033; reviewer-approved in "Phase 3 contract decisions", Q3, Q4 `singleTab`, Q11 and the addendum, plus what the step needed beyond that list).** Problem: the lock port could not express a wait (promotion) or a browser without Web Locks, there was no change-channel contract, boot could not report a single-tab session, and the store had no way for a follower tab to mirror the writer: `replaceState` refuses while read-only, validates the whole state on every call and assigns revision + 1. Change: `WriterLock` gains `whenAcquired()` and the acquire result `'unsupported'`; new `ChangeChannel { post, subscribe, close }` and `ChangeMessage { fromRevision, revision, changedIds }` (`changedIds` may be `'all'`, `subscribe` hands out `unknown`); `BootReport.singleTab` (required); `CanonicalStore` gains `applyRemote(RemoteChange)` and `reload(state, revision)`, allowed only on a read-only store (`CommandErrorCode 'NOT_FOLLOWER'` otherwise), `ChangeSet.cause` gains `'remote'` (documented as possibly spanning revisions; the core index engine applies a remote ChangeSet incrementally as a forward span, every other cause keeps the +1 guard); adapter additions `readChanged`, `loadForFollower`, `promote`, option `channel`; `PersistenceEvent 'channel_error'`; `attachWriteQueue`. Guards, semantics and rejected alternatives: ADR-033. No schema change, no `DB_STORES`/`DB_VERSION` change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-033, ARCHITECTURE.md §2.4, PERSISTENCE.md §4 and §10, SONNET_HANDOFF.md §9.
- **2026-09-28, step 3.3 review — boot quarantine is a move; `BootReport.quarantineTruncated` removed; `QuarantineRow.raw` documented per source (reviewer decision; contract and doc changes).** Problem: step 3.3 quarantined a bad boot row as a COPY (the row stayed in `records`, the quarantine row's raw value was capped at 64 Ki characters). `records` then differed from the in-memory state, so `replaceAll`, a snapshot restore or a revision-gap recovery would silently delete a row that was only ever kept there, and a capped copy could not stand in for it (CLAUDE.md non-negotiable 5). Change: a bad row is removed from `records` and written to `quarantine`, whole and exactly as stored, in one transaction through `runOrdered` (`BootPorts.moveToQuarantine` replaces `addQuarantine`; `assembleState` returns `RejectedRow`s). The 64 Ki cap (`QUARANTINE_RAW_MAX_CHARS`, `QuarantineRow.rawTruncated`) stays for display and import quarantine (3.6); `rawTruncated` is never set on boot rows. `BootReport.quarantineTruncated` is removed because it is always 0; `newlyQuarantined` counts the rows moved. `QuarantineRow.raw` and `rawTruncated` comments say which source they apply to. No schema change, no `DB_STORES`/`DB_VERSION` change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §4, §7. Also in the same commit (test tooling, no contract change): wall-clock assertions moved to `npm run test:timing` (TEST_STRATEGY §1, SONNET_HANDOFF §19, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN 3.12).
- **2026-09-28, step 3.3 — boot: quarantine rows, import batches, asset-blob IDs, `OpenResult` (`migration_failed`, `detail`, `bootReport`), and what the step needed beyond the approved list (contract changes, reviewer-approved in "Phase 3 contract decisions", Q1 a, b, e, Q4, Q10).** Problem: boot (PERSISTENCE.md §4) had nowhere to put rows that fail L1, no way to report a failed migration or what it found, no asset-blob list for AST-002, and no way to tell a crashed session from a clean one. Change: `PersistenceAdapter` gains `addQuarantine` (ordered; rows whose ID exists are kept; returns the IDs that were new), `listQuarantine`, `deleteQuarantine`, `saveImportBatch`, `listImportBatches`, `listAssetBlobIds`; `ImportBatchRow` (report as opaque `JsonValue`, no io import); `QuarantineRow.rawTruncated` and `QUARANTINE_RAW_MAX_CHARS` (64 Ki characters); `OpenResult` (`readOnlyReason: 'migration_failed'`, `detail`, `bootReport: BootReport`); `setLastCleanShutdownAt(at: string | null)` (`null` = a session is running); `SaveStatus.failureReason?: 'revision_gap'` (step 3.2 review decision); `replaceAll` documented as ordered, resyncing and refusing a stale state. Test support: `withVersionOffset` (Q10). Also an implementation change to step 3.2's `decodeSnapshot`: it accepts any numeric `schemaVersion` (the runner decides usability), so a snapshot of data the runner refuses reports the runner's reason instead of "corrupt". `DexieAdapterOptions` (implementation options, not contract) gains `lock`, `runner`, `uiStateValidators`. No schema change, no `DB_STORES`/`DB_VERSION` change (the quarantine, importBatches, editorSessions and uiState stores already existed), no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §2, §4 (rewritten), §7.
- **2026-09-28, step 3.2 — snapshots: `readSnapshot` returns the tolerant document, `SaveStatus.deletablePeriodicSnapshotIds`, restore types (contract changes, reviewer-approved in "Phase 3 contract decisions", Q1 (f), Q2, Q9).** Problem: an older snapshot has to be migrated before it can be parsed strictly, so `readSnapshot(): Promise<DatasetDocument>` could not serve restore; the quota sequence's stage 2 (PERSISTENCE.md §11) had no place to report the periodic snapshots the user may delete; restore needed named result and port types. Change: `readSnapshot` returns `SnapshotReadResult` (`{ ok: true, summary, doc: MigratableDocument }` or `{ ok: false, code: 'NOT_FOUND' | 'CORRUPT', message }`; the wrapper beyond the approved wording is there because damage is a value, CLAUDE.md §6); `SaveStatus.deletablePeriodicSnapshotIds?`; `RestoreSnapshotResult`; doc comments on `createSnapshot`/`listSnapshots`/`deleteSnapshot` (write queue order, newest first, pruning). `DexieAdapterOptions` (an implementation option, not contract) gains a required `ids`. No schema change, no `DB_STORES`/`DB_VERSION` change (a snapshot row is the summary plus a Blob), no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §2, §9, §11.
- **2026-09-28, step 3.1 review — INV-20, PERSISTENCE.md §1/§3 and §10 wording (doc wording, no contract change).** Problem: INV-20 and PERSISTENCE.md said "one transaction per commit", while step 3.1 (reviewer-approved) lets ChangeSets queued together share one transaction. Change: every IndexedDB transaction is atomic and ends on a committed revision; an idle queue writes one transaction per ChangeSet; ChangeSets queued together may share one transaction ending on the newest. Also recorded in the same commit: every non-ChangeSet IndexedDB write (markers, snapshots) runs through the queue's ordering (`runOrdered`) and `derivedCache` is the single documented exception (PERSISTENCE.md §2/§3); the change-channel message is `{ fromRevision, revision, changedIds }` (PERSISTENCE.md §10, 3.4 Must cover cell; the type itself lands in 3.4).
- **2026-09-28, step 3.1 — `PersistenceAdapter`: derived cache, meta markers, asset blobs in the write, `getStatus` (contract changes, reviewer-approved in "Phase 3 contract decisions").** Problem: the write side needed (Q1 c) a place for `derivedCache` rows and their LRU eviction (quota stage 1 has to evict something), (Q1 d) setters for the clean-shutdown and integrity-check markers, (Q1 asset write) one transaction over an asset record and its bytes (INV-20; "blob first, then the record" was rejected), and (Q2) a synchronous pending-writes query for `beforeunload`. Change, all additive except one removal: `DerivedCacheRow`, `DerivedCacheInput`, `DerivedPutResult`, `putDerived`, `getDerived`; `setLastCleanShutdownAt`, `setLastIntegrityCheckAt`; `AssetBlobWrite` and `writeChanges(changes, next, blobs?)` (the third parameter is optional; `next` stays, see step 3.1); `getStatus()`; **`putAssetBlob` removed** (a standalone blob write is the rejected path; blobs of deleted asset records are kept for undo, an orphan sweep is a Phase 9 Diagnostics action). Docs updated in the same commit: PERSISTENCE.md §2, §3, §5, §11, SONNET_HANDOFF.md §9. No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change.
- **2026-09-28, step 2.13 — `FindingStore.replaceForRules`, `Validator.validateTouchedByRule` (with `TouchedValidation`, `commitTouched`) and `ValidationRule.peerReads` (contract changes, ADR-032; all additive, reviewer-approved).** Problem: after ADR-031 a hub edit that changes a field its referrers read (a species' `review`, RVW-002) still re-ran every referrer whole (~50 ms of a 67-77 ms commit on the like-water hub), and touching a class re-ran SPC-006 on every species listing it (1,400 at the design tier; ~87 ms per commit), although one rule of about forty was affected. Change: `FindingStore.replaceForRules(subjects, ruleIds, findings)` replaces only the listed rules' findings for each subject (the whole-subject `replaceForSubjects` stays); `Validator.validateTouchedByRule(state, indexes, touched, previous, known)` returns the subjects re-validated whole plus, per rule set, the referrers and peers of which only the rules that read what changed (or that named them) were re-run, and the command store makes that one call per commit (the doubled `scopeOf` call is gone); an affected L2 rule keeps a record whole (an L2 ERROR gates its L3 rules), and a per-rule subject with an L1/L2 ERROR in the store is skipped. `peerReads` (the mirror of `referencedReads`: fields of a touched record a rule reads when validating one of its other records) makes `peers` field-aware; undeclared means called on every touch. `scopeOf`/`validateTouched` keep their meaning. No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-032, VALIDATION_RULES.md §5, ARCHITECTURE.md §2.4, PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §3, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md (§1 item 7, Phase 4 note), TEST_STRATEGY.md §6 (general performance rule for gates).
- **2026-09-28, step 2.12 — PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §2/§4 wording (doc only, no contract change).** The "Commit on a record with >= 500 referrers" row is annotated as a backstop (the hub scenarios it was added for now meet the 16 ms Commit row) and kept for Phase 9; §4's incremental-validation bullet describes field-aware scope. Reason: the step 2.12 re-measure (see step entry).
- **2026-09-28, step 2.12 — `ValidationRule.referencedReads` (contract change, ADR-031).** Problem: `Validator.scopeOf`/`validateTouched` added every referrer of a touched record, at record-kind granularity, so renaming a species used by 1,000 reactions re-validated 1,000 reactions although no rule of theirs reads a species' name (measured 65-94 ms per commit at the design tier against the 16 ms budget; step 2.10 found that a fix needed a rule-level field declaration and added a 50 ms row instead; the reviewer then approved this amendment). Change: `ValidationRule` gains an optional `referencedReads?: ReferencedReads` (per kind of referenced record, or `'*'`, the top-level fields the rule reads; `[]` = existence only). `scopeOf` keeps a referrer only if a rule of its kind reads a field that changed (diffed by reference against `previous`), or the record was created or deleted; a rule without the declaration re-runs on any change, and without `previous` nothing is diffed. Only the referrer expansion is narrowed; referents and `peers`/`watches` are unchanged. `Validator`'s interface is unchanged (`previous` was already there). No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-031, VALIDATION_RULES.md §4-§5, ARCHITECTURE.md §2.4, PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §4.
- **2026-09-28, step 2.11 — PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §1, §9, §10 (benchmark method and tier definition; no contract, schema or invariant change).** Problem: (1) the design-tier navigator benchmark anchored a class-count ratio, so its hub count moved with the generator and was never a stated number; (2) single-run, low-sample benchmarks gave p99 values that were the sample max and varied 3× between runs of identical code, and a first fix that ran the repeats in one process was itself biased by JIT warm-up. Change: §1 states 60 anchored hubs at the design tier and 400 at the stress tier (judged against the ≤ 2× allowance, 32 ms informal); §9 documents isolation (PID lock), explicit warmup/100-sample floor, 3 fresh-process runs with median p99 and spread; §10 documents the deterministic anchor rule. Benchmark tooling and doc wording only.
- **2026-09-27, step 2.10 — PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §2's Commit row covers rejected commits too (doc
  wording, no contract change).** Problem: a rejected commit paid for a full `O(dataset)` index rebuild
  (`core/commands/store.ts`, `coreIndexEngine.build(state, revision)`), which §4 explicitly forbids and §3
  puts on the main thread where §2 allows no task over 50 ms — measured at 163–580 ms at the design tier
  (step 2.8/2.9), 10–35× the 16 ms budget. Change: the rejection path now rolls the indexes back by
  applying the inverse ChangeSet through the same incremental `apply` path (`coreIndexEngine.apply`'s
  revision-gap guard now also accepts exactly one step *backward*, not only forward, and runs the same
  retract-then-add mechanics either way — `src/core/indexes/engine.ts`); §2's "Commit" row is reworded to
  "Commit, accepted or rejected" rather than adding a second row, since the same 16 ms budget applies to
  both. No interface changed (`CoreIndexEngine`/`IndexEngine` keep the same `{build, apply}` shape); this is
  a behavioural fix within `apply`'s existing signature, not even an additive contract change. Measured
  163–580 ms → 12.7–23.3 ms (mean now under 16 ms on both design-tier shapes; p99 close but not yet
  reliably under it — see PROGRESS.md step 2.10 for the full before/after). Docs updated in the same
  commit: PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §2.
- **2026-09-27, step 2.9 — `ReadonlyIndexes.navigatorRollup()` (contract change, ADR-030).** Problem:
  the navigator projection scanned every reaction in the state on every `build()` call (measured 21.81–27.2
  ms at the design tier against the 16 ms budget, Gate B packet §5; worse on p99 — this step's own commit 2
  found 79–103 ms p99 before the fix), because hub resolution and candidate extraction were recomputed from
  scratch every call regardless of whether the state had changed. Change: `ReadonlyIndexes` gains
  `navigatorRollup(): NavigatorRollupIndex` (`src/core/indexes/navigator-rollup.ts`) — every reaction's
  hub-level contribution, aggregated per hub, maintained incrementally by `IndexEngine.apply` (touched
  reactions recomputed; a class-membership change recomputes only referencing reactions; an
  anchor/class-hierarchy/display change rebuilds this one index). Hub resolution itself moved from
  `core/graph/navigator/hubs.ts` into `core/indexes` (a cycle otherwise: `core/graph` already imports
  `core/indexes`); `core/graph/navigator/{hubs,select}.ts` are now thin wrappers, so every existing caller's
  signature is unchanged. No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture
  change. Docs updated in the same commit: GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §8/§10, ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-030.
- **2026-09-27, step 2.6 — `EdgeSemantic: 'uses_mechanism'`, and classification edges from a reaction drop `reactionId` (contract changes, ADR-029).** Problem: (1) the `mechanisms` view needed an edge semantic for "this reaction/outcome links this mechanism" and none of the existing ones fit (`docs/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md §2` step 2.6); (2) the projection harness's INV-26 check is deliberately endpoint-independent for any edge that carries `reactionId` ("no two edges claim the same one, whatever their endpoints" — GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §10), which is correct for a participation/production but wrong for a *classification* edge from a reaction (topic mapping, named-reaction family, reaction-level mechanism link) — a reaction can validly carry several of any of these, and the harness caught the collision the first time `syllabus` ran over the demo's one cross-chapter reaction. Change: `EdgeSemantic` (`src/core/graph/contracts.ts`) gains `'uses_mechanism'`; `membership` edges from a reaction (`named_reactions`, `syllabus`) and reaction-level (no `outcomeId`) `uses_mechanism` edges (`mechanisms`) no longer set `GraphEdge.reactionId` — the reaction stays recoverable from `edge.from`. Outcome-level `uses_mechanism` edges keep `reactionId` (an outcome names at most one mechanism, so `outcomeId` already guarantees uniqueness); consumes/produces/transforms_to/uses_reagent are unaffected. No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §7, ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-029.
- **2026-09-27, step 2.5 — `EdgeSemantic: 'overflow'`, `DEFAULT_DEGREE_CAP`, and a required `view` parameter on the reaction-explorer edge builders (contract changes).** Problem: (1) a degree-cap overflow edge stands for several collapsed neighbours, never one real participation/production, so labelling it `consumes`/`produces` would be misleading and would need a `reactionId` it does not have (docs/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md §2 step 2.5, ADV-012/ADV-052); (2) reusing `reaction_explorer`'s `consumesEdge`/`producesEdge`/`fromReaction`/`fromSubstance` for `precursor_explorer` unchanged baked `view: 'reaction_explorer'` into every edge ID, which is wrong (GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §10 makes `view` part of the edge-ID identity) and was caught immediately by the projection harness's own INV-26 check ("not the hash of its identity") the first time the new tests ran. Change: `EdgeSemantic` (`src/core/graph/contracts.ts`) gains `'overflow'`; `DEFAULT_DEGREE_CAP = 12` added next to `DEFAULT_TRAVERSAL_LIMITS`; `consumesEdge`, `producesEdge`, `fromReaction`, `fromSubstance` (`src/core/graph/reaction-explorer/{select,neighbours}.ts` — internal to `src/core/graph`, not part of any other module's public contract) now take a required `view: ViewKey` parameter instead of a hardcoded constant. No schema, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §7/§11, ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-028.
- **2026-09-27, step 2.4 — `ProjectionRequest.conditionVariantId` (contract change, ADR-027).** Problem: the reaction explorer must let a caller narrow one focused reaction to a single condition variant (docs/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md §2 step 2.4, ADV-008), but `ViewFilters` (`src/core/schema/presentation.ts`) is persisted by `view_preset` and names a cross-reaction restriction (topics, reagent families, scope) — a condition variant instead names a part *within* one reaction, so it does not belong there. Change: `ProjectionRequest` (`src/core/graph/contracts.ts`, a runtime type, not a Zod schema) gains an optional `conditionVariantId?: ConditionVariantId`; `kit.ts`'s `projectionKey` hashes it so two selections never share a cache key. No schema, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Docs updated in the same commit: GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §7, ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS.md ADR-027.
- **2026-09-26, step 1.10 — `MigrationRunner.run` returns a result value (contract change).** Problem: the contract said the runner "rejects newer versions" but returned only `{ doc, steps }`, leaving no value to reject with (CLAUDE.md §6: errors across module boundaries are values). Change: `run` returns `MigrationResult = { ok: true, doc, steps } | { ok: false, code, message, atVersion }`, with new `MigrationErrorCode`, `MigrationError`, `MigrationSuccess` types; `MigrationRunner` gains a readonly `target`. No consumer existed yet, no record shape changed: no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, migration or fixture change. Docs updated: MIGRATION.md §2.
- **2026-09-26, step 1.8 — validation contract, `linkersOf`, L2 nested-container rule.** Problem: incremental validation must equal a full pass, but records can be affected by changes elsewhere that the reverse-reference index does not show (name twins, duplicate IDs, cycle members, rich-text linkers, routes depending on species and classes), and a fixed problem on an untouched record must lose its finding. Change: `ValidationRule` gains optional `watches` and `peers` (records beyond referrers/referents affected by a touched record); `Validator.validateTouched` gains an optional `previous` state and the interface gains `scopeOf(state, indexes, touched, previous?)`; `CoreIndexes.linkersOf(id)` (rich-text link index, apply ≡ build tested). Separately, step 1.7's REF-005 no longer reports a nested reference whose container field names no record of the required kind (participant `profileId` on a non-reagent): that case is RXN-011 as VALIDATION_RULES.md §3 lists it. No record shape changed: no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, migration or fixture change. Docs updated: VALIDATION_RULES.md §4–§5, GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §8.
- **2026-09-26, step 1.7 — indexes `definersOf`, validation contracts on `CoreIndexes`, rich-text registry (additive).** Problem: REF-006 (nested ID defined twice, INV-01) needs to know which other records define a nested ID, and the docs forbid scanning all records; `RuleContext.indexes` and `Validator` required `ReadonlyIndexes`, which nobody can build before step 2.1, so no rule could run; REF-008 needs to know where rich text lives, and no registry said. Change: `CoreIndexes.definersOf(id)` (nested ID → defining records, maintained by `apply` like the other buckets, covered by the apply ≡ build test); `RuleContext.indexes` and `Validator.validateTouched/validateAll` take `CoreIndexes` (`ReadonlyIndexes` extends it, so callers with the full set are unaffected); new `RICH_TEXT_PATHS` in `src/core/schema/rich-text.ts` with a test that derives the expected list from the schemas via the maximal fixture. No record shape changed: no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, migration or fixture change. Docs updated: VALIDATION_RULES.md §4, GRAPH_SEMANTICS.md §8.
- **2026-09-26, step 1.6 — `src/core/indexes/contracts.ts` split (additive).** Problem: step 1.6 delivers only part of `ReadonlyIndexes`; step 2.1 delivers the rest, and a partial object cannot satisfy the full interface. Change: new `CoreIndexes` (revision, `get`, `referrersOf`, `byFoldedName`, `classAncestors` plus new `ancestorsOf`/`descendantsOf` over `HierarchyKind`) that `ReadonlyIndexes` extends; new `CoreIndexEngine`; `IndexEngine.build` gains an optional `revision` (default 0); documented that `apply` invalidates the `previous` handle. All existing members keep their signatures. No schema change, no version bump, no ADR affected.
- **2026-09-26, step 1.2a — `SafeUrlSchema` and embedded-asset base64 tightened.** Problem: `SafeUrlSchema` accepted control/bidi characters (the URL parser normalises them, but the raw string was stored), and `embeddedAssets[].base64` had only a length cap. Change: `SafeUrlSchema` gains the single-line forbidden-character refinement; new `Base64Schema` (alphabet, padding, length % 4 = 0, non-empty, cap `LIMITS.embeddedAssetBase64` = 28,000,000, unchanged) replaces the bare string. Only tightens validation: every previously valid, non-hostile value is still valid, so no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump or migration (MIGRATION.md §1). Existing schema fixtures and the legacy fixtures (which contain no URLs or base64) are unaffected. Invalid fixtures and tests added. No ADR affected.
- **2026-09-26, step 1.1 — `src/core/schema/primitives.ts` lint exceptions.** Problem: ESLint `no-control-regex` flags `FORBIDDEN_MULTILINE` / `FORBIDDEN_SINGLE_LINE`, which intentionally match control characters. Change: two line-scoped `eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex` comments with a justification. Validated shape is byte-identical, so no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, migration or fixture freeze. No ADR affected.
- **2026-10-02, Phase 5 contract decisions (reviewer).** DECIDED, NOT APPLIED: each contract, schema-adjacent or lint amendment listed in "Phase 5 contract decisions (reviewer, 2026-10-02)" lands, with its own entry here, in the step that item names (5.0 warm-up and boundaries; 5.1b derived formula, `SearchSync.adopt` / `refresh`; 5.3 Scene `truncation` and `SceneNode.state`; 5.4c `ProjectionDefinition.readsKinds`; 5.5b `LayoutServiceOptions.persist`). Reason: the plan review's fit scan (Q1-Q14). No source changed in this commit.
- **2026-10-02, step 5.0 — engine warm-up (contract decision Q7).** Problem: the 2 s limit of the chem and layout workers also covered the engine's cold start, so on a slow device the first call could time out, restart the worker, and three of those in a row end in `gave_up` (ADR-034; the same problem as the battery failures of the last three sessions). Smallest change: additive `warmUp(params: {})` on `ChemWorkerApi` and `LayoutWorkerApi` (`WarmUpParams`, `WarmUpResult`, `ENGINE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS` = 20,000 in `src/workers/rpc/contracts.ts`), additive optional `LayoutEngine.warmUp?()` in `src/layout/contracts.ts`, `createChemHandlers(toolkit, clock)` and `startChemHost(port, load, clock)` take the injected clock, `createChemService(client, gate)` and `LayoutServiceOptions.gate` take a `WarmUpGate`. No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration. ADR-034 and PERFORMANCE §3 / §7 updated with dated lines.
- **2026-10-07, step 5.4c — navigator scale and expansion (contract amendment, decision Q3, ADR-036).** Problem: at 1,000 reactions the hub overview was cut at 800 raw edges, a bundle did not know its real size, which arrows survived depended on IDs, and all arrows of a hub onto itself were one self-loop bundle (step 5.0 fact sheet, reviewer item 7). Smallest change: (1) additive `EdgeBundle.total` (required; `kit.bundleEdges` sets `edgeIds.length`), `Truncation.hiddenPairs` / `hiddenEdges` (optional), `GraphNode.hiddenMembers` (optional, overflow nodes), `ProjectionDefinition.readsKinds` (optional; undefined = rebuild on every commit); (2) a pure `sameProjection` in `core/graph`; (3) `NavigatorRollupIndex.pairs()` and `NavigatorCandidate.substrate` / `product` (`core/indexes`, additive); (4) the navigator semantics per ordered node pair and hub expansion, as GRAPH_SEMANTICS §3, §7, §8, §10, §11 now say (dated 2026-10-07). No schema change, no `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration, no fixture change. Existing tests that encoded the cut at `maxEdges` are listed in the step 5.4c section.

- 2026-10-07, step 5.4b (additive, no schema change): `src/render/svg/contracts.ts` gains `SceneSvgProps.svgRef`, `SceneSvgProps.interactive` and `DepictionSource.cancel?` (all optional); the depiction manager gains `cancel` and treats an answer with reason `BUSY` as not cached. Reason: the viewport binds gestures to the root `<svg>`, cancels depiction requests that left the screen, and a busy service must not show as unavailable.
- 2026-10-08, step 5.5a part 1 (additive, no schema change): `AppServices.logApp(type, message)` adds one line to the event log as source "app" (the UI's way to leave a trace; nothing is silent). The navigator feature's port carries the UI stores' persistence (the app wires it to `uiState` in part 2). Approved by the reviewer in the 5.5a interim review.

- 2026-10-08, step 5.5a part 3 / readability (additive, no schema change): `LayoutEdgeInput.label?` (one label size) and `LayoutResult.labelPositions?` (top-left of each label box); the layout cache key text is `layout/2` and includes the label sizes; `checkRequest` refuses a label that is not a finite size of at least 1; ELK layered sends the label as an edge label (CENTER, not inline, MEDIAN_LAYER, `elk.spacing.edgeLabel` 4) and routes ORTHOGONAL (ADR-007 dated line); the label text sent to ELK is `#` because ELK ignores a label with empty text (measured); the fallback layout places no label and says so in its notes. `SceneOptions.labelSizes?`, `labelSizes()`, `LayoutRequestOptions.labelSizes?`, `EdgeStyle.viaMembership`; `SceneEdge.labelAt` is now the CENTRE of the label box (the middle of the route in the fallback). The svg root carries `data-scene-width` and `data-scene-height` (the e2e reads the bounds). `ArrowTextContext.describe` for a reagent: its formula is present only when the reagent is backed by one species. `PANEL_DEFAULTS.inspectorOpen` is false. `NavigatorPorts` unchanged.

### Step 5.4b review (reviewer, 2026-10-07)

Verdict: ACCEPTED. Session start (the step 5.5a session): `git status` clean, HEAD 1dc2946. The AC power check is retired and was not run.
1. Approved as built: viewport store, controller (one transform per frame), LOD rule (5% hysteresis, level 1 over the budget), grid index, constants (scale 0.05 to 4, step 1.25, fit padding 24, focus cap 1.5, overscan 0.5, still time 250 ms), d3-zoom 3.0.0 with d3-selection 3.0.0 as a direct pin, additive svg contract changes.
2. The depiction queue holds 4 in flight (same as services.depictions). The retry-count fix is accepted.
3. Decision 2 (the whole Scene stays mounted; culling feeds the LOD budget and the depiction candidates) departs from prompt item e and UI_ARCHITECTURE §6 ('only those mount'). ACCEPTED for Phase 5: a Scene is bounded by the projection limits (400 / 800). Next time a departure from a prompt item is a REVIEW REQUIRED line, not a decision line. (Follow-up b.)
4. Not proved in 5.4b: build:smoke with d3 inside a chunk, and any real browser. Both are step 5.5a part 1.
5. Arrow-text conditions DECIDED (5.4a review follow-up b; the lines here win): side BELOW the arrow, after the participant parts of the same stage, same line; at most 3 condition parts a side (MAX_CONDITION_PARTS), each side ends with at most ONE `more` part counting everything not shown; values are the stage's own fields, overridden field by field by the arrow's condition variant only when the arrow has exactly ONE variant and it overrides that stage; order: temperature, pressure, light, medium, time, activation, atmosphere, anhydrous, other; `air` is written; medium is written (enum word with spaces + ' medium'); negative numbers use U+2212; a range with a negative bound is 'a to b unit', otherwise 'a-b unit' with an en dash; a gas of an atmosphere is a `formula` part; free `text` only when the stage gave no structured part, first line, at most 48 characters, visible ellipsis. Not on the arrow: solventCharacter, parsedFields, originalText, stage label and kind, variant label and note. New openItem: solventCharacter and the length of one arrow-text part are looked at again after the user's look. The 5.4a decision (2) stays until that look.
6. Step 5.5a design decisions (also in the plan row): (1) the navigator's default maxEdges is set by measurement: the largest of 800, 400, 240, 120 whose ELK mean at design dense is under 300 ms, else 120; maxNodes stays 400. (2) A node whose record has a depiction key gets a taller box, fixed at size time at every LOD: structure area 128 x 80 px above the name line; the name stays at LOD 3, the formula line gives way; species and member boxes only. (3) No layer hint and no order hint for hubs. (4) No exam-scope selector before 5.8: active exam version defaults to none, scopeOf answers `unmapped` for all. (5) Nothing on screen goes stale: the projection memo does not cover what the Scene ports read; 5.5a item d fixes the rule.
7. The AC power rule is retired by the user's decision.

**Follow-up a (docs).** Hits of the search (outside node_modules, dist): docs/SONNET_HANDOFF.md §19 (the AC sentence deleted; the real-thread line reworded); docs/PERFORMANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md machine conditions (text kept, dated line added); scripts/generate-baseline.mjs line 119 and tests/perf/baseline.json line 4 (a note written into the baseline's `runner` text, not a guard or an assertion: unchanged); docs/PROGRESS.md and docs/reviews/phase-4-close.md (history: unchanged). CLAUDE.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN: no hit. PROJECT_STATE openItems: none requires AC power. No script, config or test reads the power state.
**Follow-up b (docs).** UI_ARCHITECTURE §6 has a dated line for decision 3; new openItem (Gate E watch); the 5.5a plan row carries decisions 5 and 6.

### Step 5.5a interim review (reviewer, 2026-10-08; recorded from the session prompt)
1. The stop was right, and so was not committing.
2. The 5,000 ms timeout of `tests/integration/compute-import-threads.test.ts` (Vitest default) is a resource limit of a test that starts a real thread, not a product limit. Decision: every file of the `real-threads` project has the same per-test limit of 60 s (TEST_STRATEGY §1 has the dated line). No product limit, assertion or test body changes. ### Phase 5, step 5.5a, closing session, part 2 - structures (2026-10-08)

Delivered (items i to k).
i. `depictionKeyOf` (`createScenePorts(state, keyOfStructure)`): only a species record with a structure has a key (a class' generic structure, a reagent and a species without a structure have none); the key is the depiction service's own request key at the structure-area size (a hash of format, value and size), so it is known before any answer. `src/features/navigator/depictions.ts`: `createDepictionBinding(port, timers)` wires the manager of `src/render/svg` to `NavigatorPorts.depictions` (new, optional: `keyOf`, `depict`, `cancel`, `createUrl`, `revokeUrl`; `src/app/window/navigator-port.ts` fills it from `services.depictions` and makes object URLs with `URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml' }))`, revoked through the same port). The fetch goes to `services.depictions`; the key-to-structure lookup is bounded (`MAX_DEPICTION_LOOKUP` = 2,000, refreshed on use, the oldest dropped; a key that left it is answered BUSY and not cached). Answers: `queue_full` and `cancelled` are the manager's BUSY answer (not cached, asked again by the viewport queue a bounded number of times, never shown); `timeout`, `invalid`, `refused`, `bad_options` and `unavailable` are `unavailable` with that reason, cached; a timeout is NOT retried (test: five requests, one service call). `cancel` also reaches the service. The binding is made once per set of ports (a WeakMap) and lives with them; the manager's LRU (500) bounds what it holds. One line, what a user sees after Retry on the chem engine: a structure that failed stays a NAME until it leaves the 500-key cache or the page is reloaded (the manager keeps `unavailable` and nothing asks again; the L4 recheck after Retry does not reach the navigator); structures do NOT come back by themselves. Built nothing; openItem added.
j. Taller box: a node with a depiction key (species and member boxes only) has a structure area of 128 x 80 (`STRUCTURE_AREA`, gap 4) above its name line, fixed at size time, so the same box at every LOD; width at least 128 + 2 x padding. The name sits on the bottom line at every LOD (it does not jump); the formula line gives way: it is centred in the area while no structure is drawn (LOD 1 and 2, loading, unavailable) and is not drawn when the structure is. A species whose structure cannot be drawn shows its name (and formula) in the same box. The image fills the area (aspect ratio kept).
k. e2e (`tests/e2e/readability.spec.ts`, Chromium, production build): the demo hub Alcohols expanded (23 nodes); at LOD 2 zero overlaps (32 text rectangles, label over label 0, label over node 0, outside the bounds 0; 2 text rectangles a line crosses, reported); after Fit, wheel-zoom about the member Ethanol to LOD 3: `<image href="blob:...">` appears in its node (the chem worker answered), the name stays (one text), zero `securitypolicyviolation`, zero console errors, zero foreign requests. ### Step 5.5a review and navigator redesign (reviewer and user, 2026-10-09)

Verdict: step 5.5a ACCEPTED as built, with its amendments and changed tests. Session start: `git status` clean, HEAD d669b0e.
1. REVIEW REQUIRED 1, 3 (an openItem), 6 and 7: accepted. 2 and 4 are answered by the redesign below. 5: follow-up a.
2. THE REDESIGN (the user's look: one map of every family and every arrow is not studyable; organic chemistry is cyclic, so a layered map loops back over itself). The navigator becomes THREE levels of detail: (1) the overview map, families and plain arrows with their count, no arrow text, no hub expansion (curated arrangement and family colours: step 5.5c); (2) the family page (NEW, step 5.5b), one card per reaction with structures, reagents and conditions; (3) the reaction detail, the inspector of steps 5.6a and 5.6b, opened from a card. Principles in the new ADR-037 "Navigator as overview, family page and detail": progressive disclosure; nothing simplified or hidden (counts and links say where the rest is); colour for navigation, never decoration and never the only carrier of meaning; quality from spacing, readable structures, short traceable arrows, a consistent interface. Visual direction: textbook calm with soft family colours (hydrocarbons blue, oxygen compounds green, halogen compounds amber, nitrogen compounds violet) and rounded cards (the rounding comes in 5.5b item j, the colours in 5.5c).
3. Plan (IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN section 2): the old 5.5b row is now 5.5d (unchanged, minus what 5.5b and 5.5c take, plus the carried items (a) Retry and (c) fitted size); new rows 5.5b "Family page" and 5.5c "Overview arrangement and family colours" (the schema question is decided in that step's prompt). Carry item (b) of 5.5a (expansion stability) is CLOSED: the overview no longer expands. Dated lines: UI_ARCHITECTURE section 3 (rounded corners, 8 px cards and panels, 6 px controls) and section 5 (Navigator: overview, family page, detail); GRAPH_SEMANTICS section 3 (expanding a hub in the navigator is replaced by its family page; the projection's expansion stays in core). PROJECT_STATE nextStep stays 5.5b (retitled).

### Phase 5, step 5.5b, part 1 - the family study, pure core (2026-10-09)

Session start: `git status` clean, HEAD e6ce02e (the Part A commit).
1. `src/core/graph/family/` (`types.ts`, `describe.ts`, `study.ts`, `index.ts`): `familyStudy(ctx, hubId)`. Sections "Reactions of <family>" (carried substrate resolves to the hub, grouped by the family of the main product) and "Preparation of <family>" (a main product resolves to the hub, grouped by the family of the carried substrate). A reaction of a family into itself is in both sections, in the group of its own family. Groups by the anchor's `order` (the first anchor of a target, as the roll-up does), then folded name, then ID; Unclassified last; cards by folded reaction name, then ID. One card per (reaction, group): reaction ID, name, named-reaction names, review status, caveat count (`caveatsFor`), the carried substrate (a ref), the outcomes that land in the group (products = main products only, each a ref, with the selectivity word: `unspecified` has none, `mixture_component` is written "mixture component"), and the arrow text of `arrowTextOf` for that outcome's variants (the 5.4b rule). `elsewhere` counts, per other group of the same section, the outcomes of the reaction that go there ("+1 outcome: Alkenes"); it is empty in the Preparation section, where a reaction has one carried substrate and so one group. Header: counts per section and in total (a reaction in both sections counts once in the total), reaction types (names of the `reactionTypes` concepts, counts, by count then name), the partner strips `madeFrom` and `turnsInto` (group, name, reaction count).
2. Decisions made here: (a) the arrow text needs `describe` and `shownAsNode`, which the Scene ports build in `src/features/navigator/scene-ports.ts`; core cannot import them, so `family/describe.ts` repeats the same plain-text rule (display name, else canonical; formula; a reagent has a formula only when backed by one species) over the state it is given. (b) Schemes are found through `referrersOf(reactionId)`, only for reactions that are on a card, so the work stays proportional to the hub's candidates. (c) A candidate whose reaction is gone from the state is skipped (it cannot happen after a commit; the index and the state are one revision).
3. Tests: `src/core/graph/family/study.test.ts` (12: the demo - Alcohols header, "413 K" in the Ethers group and "443 K" in the Alkenes group each counting the other, PCC with ethanol to ethanal, group and card order, a family into itself in both sections (Aldehydes: aldol), Unclassified, an empty family (Alkanes), partner strips, determinism, an unknown hub answers null; two small states for the selectivity words, the elsewhere counts and the card order); `tests/property/family-study.test.ts` (1 property over generated states and anchor counts: the product lines shown in each section equal the hub's candidates exactly (nothing dropped, nothing twice), card keys unique, cards at most the candidates, counts equal the distinct reactions, elsewhere counts equal the outcomes not on the card, deterministic).
4. Bench (informal, once, alone, a throw-away test not committed; design tier, anchored with 60 hubs, 30 runs after a warm-up): dense, largest hub `cls_g-00003`: 4,694 candidates, 4,368 cards, mean 160.8 ms, max 231.5 ms. hub_explosion, largest hub `cls_g-00000`: 10,000 candidates, 10,000 cards, mean 291.8 ms, max 503.6 ms. No budget row exists for it; the page memoises the value per state revision and virtualises the cards, so it is paid once per open (a Gate E watch item: a family of 10,000 reactions blocks the main thread for about 0.3 s on open).

### Phase 5, step 5.5c, part 1 - the arrangement data (2026-10-09)

Session start: `git status` clean, HEAD fafcb54 (commit A, "phase-5.5b: review follow-ups", is 9d6ee22).

FACTS (item a). `navigator_anchor` today: `id`, `kind`, `target` (a record ref), `label?` (LabelText: the hub's display label), `order` (a finite number), and the shared presentation meta (`provenance`, `review`). `view_preset`: `id`, `kind`, `name`, `view`, `filters` (`examId?`, `examVersionId?`, `scopes`, `topicIds`, `reagentFamilies`, `text?`), `anchorIds` (the anchors a saved view shows), meta. `entity_display`: `id`, `kind`, `target`, `showAsNode` (`default` | `never`), `labelMode?`, `abbreviation?`, meta. ### Phase 5, step 5.5c, part 2 - the arranged overview and the family colours (2026-10-09)

Session: part 1 is 922c4db. Contract changes, all ADDITIVE (CLAUDE.md §7; recorded under Amendments below): `LayoutAlgorithm` += `'grid'`; `LayoutNodeInput.cell?: { column, row }`; `LayoutResult.engine` += `'grid'`; `GraphNode.arrangement?: NodeArrangement` (`cell?`, `group?`, `tone?`); `SceneNode.group?`, `SceneNode.tone?`; `FamilyStudy.group?`, `FamilyStudy.tone?`; `NavigatorState.layout` (which engine drew the Scene). The layout cache key is `layout/3` and includes the cell (the cache is memory only).
c. THE GRID LAYOUT: `src/layout/grid/` (`arrange.ts` cells and sizes, `route.ts` lines and arcs, `chips.ts` count chips, `geometry.ts`, `index.ts` = `layoutGrid`), pure, deterministic, no ELK, tested (`grid.test.ts`, 22). WHERE IT RUNS: in the layout worker, with the other layouts: `createLayoutHandlers` answers a `grid` request with `layoutGrid` before ELK is asked (and `layoutFallback` answers a grid request with the grid, whatever the reason); the main-side service does not wait for the ELK warm-up for a grid request and caches its answer (never a layout on the main thread, PERFORMANCE §11). The stray case (a `grid` request that reaches the ELK engine) is laid out layered and says so in `notes`. USED when the projection is the navigator overview, has at least one anchored hub, every anchored hub has a cell, and there is no other node but the Unclassified hub (`isArranged` in `layout-request.ts`); otherwise ELK as before. The grid request carries no `previous` and the pipeline asks once (a grid answer is already a fixed point). Columns are as wide as their widest box and rows as tall as their tallest; THE STATED GAPS: between columns 2 x `spacing.layer` (192 px by default), between rows 1.5 x `spacing.layer` (144 px) (room for a line, its chip and the chip of the reverse arrow); a box is centred in its cell; Unclassified (no cell) goes one row below the last used row, from column 0 (several boxes without a cell: one column each, id order); boxes that share a cell are stacked in it (`spacing.node` apart) and the result says so in `notes` (PRS-004 warns about the data).
d. EDGES: a straight line between the two box borders (the line from centre to centre, clipped to the boxes); the edges between the same two boxes (an arrow and its reverse) are parallel lines `PARALLEL_GAP` = 10 px apart, spread about the centre line in edge ID order. A line that would pass within 4 px of another box is a quadratic curve sampled into 24 segments (the distance of its middle from its chord is the smallest of 40, 80, 120, 160 px, either side, that clears every box; the side the parallel line was shifted to is tried first, so an arrow and its reverse bow apart); none clears: straight, and counted in `notes` ("N arrow(s) cross a box: no arc of 40 to 160 px clears the boxes, so the line stays straight"). A count chip sits at the middle of its line; if it would overlap another chip or a box (2 px margin) it slides along the line to the first free place of 0.5, 0.4, 0.6, 0.3, 0.7 of its length; none free: kept at the middle and counted in `notes`. Self-loops are drawn by the Scene as before. THE DEMO (real fonts, 1280 x 800, production build; `screenshots/arrangement-numbers.json`): 26 paths, 2 arcs (Alcohols to Carboxylic acids, 40 px bow around Aldehydes; Ketones to Unclassified), the other lines straight; of 6 count chips 2 slid (Haloalkanes to Alkenes, Aldehydes to Alcohols); ZERO lines through a box, ZERO chip overlaps, nothing outside the bounds; drawing 1,428 x 840. NO ANCHOR MOVED: the arrangement of the prompt needed no change.
e. COLOURS (tokens only): `tokens.css` has `--color-tone-<tone>-tint | -border | -ink` for the seven tones (21 tokens; the colour check stays green); `tokens.test.ts` computes the WCAG 2.2 contrast: the tone ink and the ordinary text colours (ink, ink-2) on the tint (4.5:1), the ink on the page and the surface (4.5:1), the left rule against the tint and the page (3:1), no two tones alike, and the tones are exactly the schema's. A hub card on the overview: the tint and border of the tone, a left rule in its ink clipped to the card, `rx` = 8 (`HUB_RADIUS`, a test keeps it equal to `--radius-card`); a hub without a tone and the Unclassified hub stay neutral. The rule is clipped by a shared clip path per distinct hub size in `defs.tsx` (an existing test of the clip-path count changed for that). A hub's accessible name and `<title>` include its group ("Alcohols, class hub, Oxygen compounds, ..."). THE LEGEND: `legendOf(scene)` (pure; each group of the drawing once, from the data, ordered the way the grid reads: highest first box, then leftmost) and `NavigatorLegend`, a row under the toolbar (not an overlay: Unclassified sits at the bottom left). THE FAMILY PAGE: the header takes the family's tone (tint, border, left rule), writes its group above the name, and the "Made from" / "Turns into" buttons and titles take the SAME tone (this family's, as the prompt says; the partner's own tone is a one-line change if the user prefers it).
f. Fixed on the way: the shell's layout notice said "fallback layout" for a grid answer: a grid layout is a layout of its own and is reported only when it has notes (`chem-layout.ts`, test added). `data-layout-engine` on the canvas root, `data-from` / `data-to` on an edge group (hooks for the e2e).
g. Tests (new): `grid.test.ts` 22, `legend.test.ts` 5, `navigator-arrangement.test.tsx` 5, `arrangement.test.ts` (part 1), `layout-request.test.ts` +4, `build-scene.test.ts` +2, `scene-svg.test.tsx` +5, `navigator.test.ts` +4, `study.test.ts` +1, `navigator-family.test.tsx` +2, `pipeline.test.ts` +2, `same-request.test.ts` +1, `layout.test.ts` (worker) +4, `tokens.test.ts` +15, `app-services-engines.test.ts` +1. e2e `arrangement.spec.ts` 6 (Chromium). CHANGED existing tests, with the reason: the SVG snapshots (hub clip paths, rx, groups in the accessible names, `data-from` / `data-to`; the overview is the grid layout; the expanded one is still layered); `scene-svg.test.tsx` clip-path count (one rounded clip per distinct hub size on top of the label clips); `pipeline.test.ts` (the tests of ELK's stable first view and previous positions run on the demo WITHOUT its arrangement, `layeredDemo()`, because the arranged demo takes the grid path; the arranged demo has its own tests); `navigator-reference.ts` (the independent pre-2.9 reference takes the arrangement from the same pure reading of the anchors: it is presentation, not hub resolution); `migrations-framework.test.ts` (a quadratic `new Set` inside a filter made the maximal test slow; computed once, no timeout raised); test doubles `scene-kit.ts` `pipe` and `navigator-kit.ts` answer a grid request with the grid layout.
h. Files split to stay under 400 lines: `family-header.module.css` (the header and strip styles moved out of `family-page.module.css`), `pipeline-arranged.test.ts`, `scene-svg-tone.test.tsx`. `unarrangedDemoState` (test-support) is the demo without its arrangement, for the tests of the layered path.

### Phase 5, step 5.5d, part 1 - clearer arrows on the arranged overview (2026-10-10)

Session: Part A is 9e3a1db ("phase-5.5c: review recorded"). Items a to d, tests first or alongside.
a. ENDS (`src/layout/grid/ports.ts`, new; `route.ts` rewritten around it). The side of a box an arrow uses is the one facing the other box (sideways when |dx| x (h1 + h2) > |dy| x (w1 + w2), else up or down). The arrows that share a side are clustered by the OTHER box, the clusters ordered by where that box lies along the side (then by ID), and spread evenly over the side between `CORNER_CLEAR` (10 px: the corner radius 8 and air) from each end; a cluster (an arrow and its reverse) keeps `PORT_GAP` (12 px) between its arrows, in edge ID order at both ends, so the pair runs parallel and does not cross. A side too short for its arrows keeps the 12 px, reaches into the corner clearance and says so in `notes` ("N side(s) hold more arrows than fit 12 px apart clear of the corners..."). Arcs and "no line through a box" are unchanged (an arc starts and ends at the end points; an arrow and its reverse bow apart, the side decided from where each line's middle lies). Chips slide through nine places (0.5, 0.4, 0.6, 0.3, 0.7, 0.2, 0.8, 0.15, 0.85): with the five before, one chip on the demo found no free place.
b. WEIGHT AND HOVER. Ink token for every line (it was ink-2 except heavy), 2 px for the ordinary line (`WEIGHT_WIDTH` heavy 3, normal 2, light 1.25: heavier stays heavier), a filled 10 px head in ink; dash patterns unchanged. A hovered or selected arrow: the overlay layer draws a 4 px accent path with an accent head (`markerAccent`) and outlines its two families (`data-overlay="end"`); a hovered arrow that is also selected is drawn once. The hover is ONE delegated `pointermove` / `pointerleave` listener on the svg (ignored while a button is held: a drag is a pan; reported only when the arrow changes). Render counters: a change of `hoveredId` commits the overlay layer only (unit test) and the e2e shows exactly one commit on the page for a hover.
c. CHIPS AND LOOPS. A chip is a 20 px high pill (12 px text, paper fill, `--color-ink-2` border), `role="img"` with the name and the title "N reactions" ("1 reaction"), drawn from LOD 1 (was 2: the fitted overview is at LOD 1 or more and the chips are what the user could not read). The room the layout reserves for a chip is its own size (width + 10, height 20, no vertical margin: with 28 the ELK overview of a file without cells no longer settled, see the changed test below). A self-loop is redrawn at the box's top-right corner: it leaves the top edge 12 px from the corner, swings up and out and enters the right edge 12 px below it with a filled head pointing in; its chip sits beside it; every loop has a chip (a single reaction too: it had arrow text before); the loop's name is "N reactions that stay within <family>" ("1 reaction that stays within <family>"). The loop reaches `LOOP_RISE` (40) above its box: when a box with a loop lies closer than that to y = 0 the Scene lifts everything by the difference (`lifted` in `build-scene.ts`; the layout answer is not changed), so every coordinate stays at least 0.
d. LEGEND. Under the groups, a second list "What the marks mean": a chip sample "number of reactions on this arrow", a loop sample "reactions within the same family", a dashed box "Unclassified: compounds not in any family yet"; each only when the Scene draws it (`keyOf`, pure). The samples are aria-hidden; the words carry the meaning.
THE DEMO (real fonts, 1280 x 800, production build; `screenshots/arrow-numbers.json`): 48 arrow end points on 26 box sides; every end point lies on the border of its box; the closest two on one side are 12 px apart; the nearest to a corner is 16 px; the five arrow-and-reverse pairs run 12 px apart at the middle of their lines; zero lines through a box; zero chip overlaps; zero chips outside the bounds; no anchor moved. Session: part 1 is b841721. Items e to l, tests first or alongside.
e. N = 0: the button is `unavailable` with its reason ("every reaction is in a family"). Open: a panel under the legend with a `VirtualList` of every id (folded name, then ID; a nameless reaction by its ID), each row a button with the name and the ID; a click selects the reaction in the family store (`selectReaction`, the same selection the family page uses) and the toolbar line says "Selected: reaction <name>." when nothing on the map is selected. Every row says its place in the WHOLE list (`aria-setsize` = N). Unclassified's family page is the same page as any other (nothing to build). ADV-014 UI part: unit (27 of 27 on the demo without anchors, 1,000 ids kept 1,000 and stable), component, and the e2e scrolls the real virtualised list and collects every `data-reaction-id`.
g. TRUNCATION. `truncationNoticeOf` (pure): "This map is not complete: 12 pairs of families, 40 arrows and about 5 boxes are not drawn, because <reason in words>." and "Open a family page to see everything: its full list of reactions is there, nothing is left out." shown as a notice under the toolbar. The status line (`truncationLineOf`, "Not drawn: ...") is unchanged: one plain line. Tested with the drawing limit lowered to 3 arrows (a module mock) on the demo.
h. LAYOUT CACHE. `LayoutServiceOptions.persist` (`get`, `put`; both best effort): after a memory miss the service asks the store; a stored row is used only if it carries the request's cache key, a position for every node and a route for every edge (else counted in `stats().persisted.rejected` and the worker is asked); every answer the service caches (ELK, grid, an ELK-error fallback) is offered to the store, fire and forget. The app part (`createLayoutPart`, `attachCache(adapter)` called once the database is open) keeps one `derivedCache` row of kind `layout` per cache key (`layout:` + key), only the writer tab saves (any tab reads); a refused write is `AppStatus.layoutCache = { state: 'refused', message: <reason> }`, a `layout_cache_refused` log line and the status-line word "Layout cache: not saved (quota)"; the layout itself is returned. Reads count in `layoutCache.fromStore`. Tests: `persist.test.ts` 7 (service), `app-services-layout-cache.test.ts` 3 (first run saves; a restart reads it and the worker thread is never created; a quota refusal), e2e: after a reload no `layout` request reaches any worker (the first load sent some).
i. STRUCTURES AFTER RETRY. `DepictionManager.forgetUnavailable()` forgets every `unavailable` key (listeners told; the key is idle and is asked when next shown); the navigator's depiction binding calls it when the port's `recovered` fires; the app fills `recovered` from `AppStatus.engines.chem`: it fires when the engine is `ready` after it was `unavailable` (a successful `retryEngine('chem')`), not for a first load or a failed retry. On a family page a mounted card asks again by itself; on the map the viewport queue asks at its next update (a pan or zoom). The 5.5a test that said "nothing asks again" stays true for a port without `recovered`.
j. EMPTY STATES. `emptinessOf` (pure): a dataset with no reactions ("This dataset has no reactions yet. ... Open the File menu to load the demo or to import a file with reactions."), reactions with no family on the map ("No family is set up on the map. ... every reaction is listed under Unclassified. Import a file that has them, or open Unclassified ..."), shown as notices; a family page of a family no reaction touches says so first ("<family> has no reactions in this dataset: none starts from it and none makes it. A reaction is listed here when its substrate or its product belongs to the family, so import reactions that involve its members, or check that the compounds you expect are members of it."), above the two section sentences that were already there.
k. STORAGE WARNING. The banner has "Dismiss"; the choice is `storageNoticeDismissed` in the `panels` uiState entry (saved by the panels store, tolerant read, reset and reported when damaged); the status line shows "Storage: not persistent" whenever the browser has not made storage persistent, dismissed or not. DEPARTURE (REVIEW REQUIRED 1): `persist()` is asked once after the first import, so a reloaded dataset never produced the warning and a saved dismissal would have meant nothing; at boot, in a writer tab whose dataset already has data, the app now asks `navigator.storage.persisted()` (it never prompts) and a `false` is the same answer as a refusal (`checkStorageAtBoot`, `storage-check.ts`, tested), so the warning can come back after a reload until it is dismissed.
l. TESTS and CHANGED existing tests with the reason: `panels.test.ts` and `panels-port.test.ts` (the entry also carries `storageNoticeDismissed: false`); `navigator-family.test.tsx` (the partner buttons wear their partners' tones; an Unclassified page has no tinted header but its buttons have tones); `family-items.test.ts` (a family with no reactions has the page-level sentence first); `tests/integration/app-services-status.test.ts` ("a layout fallback": a layout call now first asks the store, an IndexedDB round trip, so the test waits for the search boot's own status step before it watches). New: `notices.test.ts` 11, `navigator-completeness.test.tsx` 7, `navigator-truncation.test.tsx` 1, `navigator-port.test.ts` 2, `storage-check.test.ts` 4, `status-storage.test.tsx` 4, `persist.test.ts` 7, `app-services-layout-cache.test.ts` 3, `depictions.test.ts` +3, `study.test.ts` +1, `panels.test.ts` +1; e2e `completeness.spec.ts` 3 (Unanchored panel with axe and CSP, warm layout, dismissed warning after a reload).

### Phase 5, step 5.5d follow-up - automatic arrangement and demo reload (2026-10-10)

Session: Part A is 3392e9a. Part B, tests alongside.
a. AUTOMATIC ARRANGEMENT. `LayoutNodeInput.autoCell?: true` (additive; layout contract, cache key now `layout/4`, `checkRequest`, `sameRequest`): a hub without an authored cell is sent with it. `isArranged` is now true for every navigator overview of anchored hubs (plus Unclassified), so the grid layout draws every such overview: no ELK tangle. In the layout worker (`handlers.ts`, `src/layout/grid/auto.ts`): ELK layered (no labels, no cells, only the automatic hubs and their edges; the in-house fallback if ELK cannot answer, said in a note) draws the hubs; layers are found as groups of boxes whose x ranges overlap; column = layer, row = rank in the layer (top to bottom, then id); an authored cell is kept; an automatic cell already taken moves to the next free row of its column. Unclassified (no anchor, no `autoCell`) still goes below the grid. Deterministic; the result's `cacheKey` is that of the request that was asked; the cells live in the drawing only, never in the data (INV-11). The result says `automatic arrangement: N boxes have no cell in the data and were placed from a layered drawing (column = layer, row = rank)` (+ how many moved), which shows in the layout note; authored cells say nothing. FOUND BY THE E2E (real bug of 5.6a, fixed): a click on a hub opened the inspector pane at once; the canvas narrowed, the toolbar and the legend wrapped onto more rows and the drawing moved 56 px down, so the second click of a double click landed elsewhere and the family page never opened (the same kind of bug as the one found at 5.5d). Fix: a pick on the MAP opens the pane after `DOUBLE_CLICK_GRACE_MS` (350 ms, the injected timers); a family page opened in the meantime cancels it; a pick of a card on a family page still opens it at once. Test: `use-bridge.test.tsx` 4. CHANGED existing e2e tests, with the reason: `navigator.spec.ts` "the toolbar opens the selected family" scopes its button to the Canvas tools toolbar (the inspector has an Open family button of its own, as the unit tests already did); "the inspector says in words what arrives later" was stale (it read the 5.5a placeholder; it reads the empty state now). The 12 failures were these two plus ten dependants of the double click; after the fix navigator, family, arrangement and smoke specs (all three browsers) pass: 41 passed.
`npm run check` as ONE command: before 343 files, 5,018 tests; after 344 files, 5,024 tests (before the bridge test), green.

### Phase 5, step 5.6b, part 1 - related reactions and the knowledge kinds (2026-10-11)

Part A is dd3a349. Items c to f.
c. RELATED REACTIONS. `relatedReactionsOf(indexes, reaction)` (`src/core/graph/related`, pure over the indexes, bounded, deterministic): six groups in the order Leads on to, Comes from, Same transformation, Same named family, Shares a mechanism, Same reagent family; a group is sorted (stated before inferred, then reaction ID), lists no reaction twice, never the reaction itself, and is cut at 5,000 with `total` the whole size. `feeds_into` is stated by the exact species and inferred through a class (a species' classes and their ancestors, at most 64); an inferred entry names its class. DECISION: a by-product never counts as what a reaction makes (found on the demo: water made as a by-product by the diazotisation linked it to every hydrolysis). `shares_participant` is not shown; the reason is recorded in GRAPH_SEMANTICS §6. Never stored (INV-11). The inspector's reaction L3 shows the stored relationships first (type in words from the type's own label, both directions, reverseKind, note) and then the groups: 12 per group and "+N more" opens the whole list (VirtualList); empty groups are named in one line. Reaction L4 also carries the curriculum (item h of Part 2 builds it for every chemistry kind: it is already there).
d. MECHANISM (`view-mechanism.tsx`). L2 summary, mechanism classes, variants (steps, rate law, applicability). L3 each variant as a section with its steps as ordered rows (kinds, description, species by ref link, label or inline structure through the depiction binding, bonds formed and broken as atom-map pairs, electron movements "lone pair on atom 3 to the bond between atoms 3 and 5, 2 electrons", rate-determining, reversible, stereochemical event, note) and who uses it (reactions with their outcome, VirtualList). A reaction's outcome link opens the mechanism AT its variant: ADDITIVE change of the UI store `InspectorState.open(ref, at?)` (a history entry may carry `at`, a variant ID; the same record at another place is a new entry); the variant is open, marked "opened from a reaction" and brought into view, and L3 opens. Arrow-pushing pictures are FUTURE: rows only.
e. NAMED REACTION, CONCEPT, TRANSFORMATION, CAVEAT (`view-knowledge.tsx`): as the prompt lists; the reactions of a named reaction or concept are a direct list and a second list "under its children"; a caveat's targets are links with their role. Class view: members and the reactions that use the class are virtualised lists (L3). Species, class, reagent and the four views above carry relationships, caveats and the curriculum at L4 (`Connections`, `CurriculumFacts`; `explainScope` in `src/core/graph/scope-explain.ts` gives the REASON of a scope: an override record, or the mapped topics with their coverage; `explicitOverride` and `mappedScope` are now exported from `scope.ts`, no behaviour change).
f. TESTS. `related.test.ts` 7 (the demo as a table: the diazotisation leads on to the Sandmeyer and the phenol reactions; an inferred link names its class; the 413 K / 443 K reaction leads on to the ethene reactions and NOTHING in the demo consumes diethyl ether; same transformation, named family, reagent family; no mechanism records in the demo, so Shares a mechanism is empty), `tests/property/related-reactions.test.ts` 1 (generated states: order, determinism, bounded, no duplicates, stated leads-on equals an oracle read from the state), `inspector-knowledge.test.tsx` 11 (Cannizzaro lists the stored Aldol relationship; "+N more" and back; follow a related reaction and Back; mechanism steps; opening at a variant from an outcome; the four kinds; caveat targets), `inspector-store.test.ts` +1. Test kit: `src/test-support/inspector-kit.tsx`.

### Phase 5, step 5.6b, part 2 - the remaining kinds (2026-10-11)

Part 1 is e4bb8d2. Items g to m. CORRECTION to Part 1: the demo DOES have mechanism records (mec_aldol-ethanal with two variants, mec_sn2-haloalkane, mec_e2-haloalkane); no two reactions share one, so "Shares a mechanism" is empty there (the test comment and one test title said "no mechanism records"; a test now makes two reactions share one).
g. ROUTE (steps in order: reaction link, outcome and condition variant by their labels, "starts from available materials" or "takes the products of step N"), TOPIC (kind, parent, children and mapped records as virtualised lists), RELATIONSHIP (type, both ends, "read both ways" with the type's own two labels, reverseKind), REFERENCE (fields, a SafeUrl link, who cites it), EXAM (versions with their status in words), EXAM VERSION (status in words: "provisional, not verified" / "draft, not verified"; every unit with the coverage of each topic), TOPIC MAPPING, EXAM RELEVANCE (`view-organisation.tsx`, `view-curriculum.tsx`).
h. CURRICULUM at L4 (`curriculum-facts.tsx`, shown for species, class, reagent, reaction, named reaction, concept, transformation, caveat, mechanism and route): the topic mappings and, for EVERY exam version of the data (the first 50, said when more), the derived scope in words (official, prerequisite, enrichment, outside the syllabus, unmapped) with the reason (an override record as a link, or the mapped topics with the version's coverage, a parent topic marked as such, or "prerequisite of an official record", or "mapped to no topic") and the version's status. `explainScope` has a test over every record and version of the demo and the maximal fixture (the scope equals `createScopeOf`'s; each reason is backed by data).
i. ASSET (`view-asset.tsx`): image, role, alt text, caption, what it is attached to, metadata. The image only through `<img>` with a blob URL made and revoked through the NEW port `InspectorPorts.assets` (additive: `open(assetId, mediaType)` answers a blob URL or a reason; `revoke(url)`; the app's port reads `persistence.getAssetBlob` and makes the URL with the record's media type, so an SVG is an image and never markup); the URL is revoked when the view goes. A missing blob says "The bytes of this image are not stored (finding AST-002)"; a tab with no storage or no port says so. Tested with an SVG asset (ADV-033 asset part): `<img src="blob:...">`, no svg/object/embed/iframe/script in the page, revoked on unmount.
j. PRESENTATION (`view-presentation.tsx`): navigator anchor (family, label, order, cell in words, group, tone), reaction scheme (reaction, variant, stage numbers, participant and product placements as links with their position), saved view (view, filters, anchors), display choice.
k. LONG LISTS: `RecordList` (`record-list.tsx`) is a `VirtualList` for every list that can be long: a class' members and its reactions (the class OR any member, at most 20,000 members scanned, said when cut), a species' reactions (new), the reactions of a named reaction, concept, transformation, a topic's children and records, a reference's citers, a caveat's targets, an asset's attachments, an exam's versions, a unit's topics, the groups of related reactions. Unit test on a hub_explosion file: the class with the most reactions (at least 1,000) shows at most 60 rows and every row says its place of the whole.
l. BREAKPOINTS: e2e at 1100 px (overlay drawer) and 600 px (bottom sheet): a pick opens the pane, a record link is reached and followed by keyboard, Alt+Left, axe, Esc closes it. FOUND BY AXE and fixed: (1) record links in lists were under 24 px (`target-size`): `.recordLink` is now an inline block of 24 px; (2) at 600 px the status bar scrolls sideways and axe says a scrollable region must be keyboard reachable (`scrollable-region-focusable`): the status bar is now a tab stop (`tabIndex 0`, a focus ring). That adds ONE tab stop to every screen (REVIEW REQUIRED 2).
m. COMPLETENESS (`inspector-kinds.test.tsx`, 71): `READABLE_KINDS` equals the 22 kinds of the schema; the maximal fixture has a record of each; for every record (all 61) the view says its kind (`data-view`), has facts at L2 and renders with every level open without a React warning (the check found a real 5.6a defect: a `dt` inside a `dd`, from a note inside a fact of a species property, a product, an intermediate and a reagent profile; the note is now an `InlineNote`); with the raw record at L5 (proved to parse back to the stored record by `inspector-completeness.test.tsx`, unchanged) no leaf is left out. Hostile text in the new views is inert. e2e `inspector-views.spec.ts` 5: Cannizzaro from its card, its related reactions, the stored link to the Aldol reaction, that reaction's mechanism at its variant, Back and Alt+Left; the curriculum in words; the drawer and the sheet; the 1,000-reaction class (at most 60 rows in the DOM while it scrolls); axe at each view and zero policy violations. Screenshots (1280 x 800): `screenshots/inspector-related-1280x800.png` (a reaction at L3 with related reactions), `screenshots/inspector-mechanism-1280x800.png` (a mechanism view).
